<script type="text/javascript"> 
    
    function facebook_login(){ 
        FB.login(function(response){
            send_response_to_server(response)
        }, {scope:"email, public_profile, user_friends, user_birthday, user_education_history, user_location"}
       )
    }

    function facebook_connect(){
        FB.getLoginStatus(function(response) {

            if (response.status == "connected") {
                send_response_to_server(response);
            } else {
                facebook_login();
            }   

        });
    }

    function send_response_to_server(response){

        var ajax_data = {"access_token" : response.authResponse.accessToken,
                         "sig"          : response.authResponse.signedRequest,
                         "id"           : response.authResponse.userID}

        $.ajax({
            type:"POST",
            url: "/ar/facebook/facebook_connect/",
            dataType: "json",
            data: ajax_data,
            success: function(msg){
                window.location = "/ar/facebook/facebook_redirect/";
            }
        })
    }

    window.fbAsyncInit = function() {
        FB.init({
            appId: '1716297215275184',
            status : true,
            cookie: true,
            xfbml : true,
            channelURL : '/ar/facebook/channel.html',
            version    : 'v2.5'
        })
    };
    (function(d){
        var js, id = 'facebook-jssdk', ref = d.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
        if (d.getElementById(id)) {return;}
        js = d.createElement('script'); js.id = id; js.async = true;
        js.src = "//connect.facebook.net/en_US/all.js";
        ref.parentNode.insertBefore(js, ref);
    }(document));
</script>

<script>
                function check_captcha(form) {
                    var msg = "Etes vous un être humain ?";

                    var r = grecaptcha.getResponse();
                    if (r.length != 0) {
                        // done
                    } else {
                        var $field = $("#captcha-resetpwd");
                        var $msg = $('<span class="errorlist ltr">' + msg + '</span>');
                        $msg.insertBefore($field);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
	/*
		infinite scroll/load */
	var escaped = false;
	$(window).load(function(){
		infinite.init("#all-articles", {
			handler: $("#load-handler"),
			url: "/ar/article/getarticles/",
			template: "#template-regular-article",
			start: 1,
			before_append: function(){
				/*
					no */
			},
			success: function(){
				this.$container.find(".col-one-half").each(function(index){
					if(index%2!=0 && !$(this).next().hasClass("clear")){
						$("<div class='clear'/><div class='m-top-15'/>").insertAfter($(this));
					}
				});
				this.$handler
					.removeClass("activate");
			},
			fail: function(){
				/*
					empty */
			},
			stop: function(){
				return true;
			},
			/*
				fire when the handler is reached */
			reach: function($handler){
				var self = this;
				if( this.$handler.hasClass("end") ){
					return;
				}
				this.$handler
					.addClass("activate");
				this.fetch({ mode: 1 });
				if(!escaped){
					this.unbind();
					self.$handler.addClass("to-action");
					this.bind = function(){
						self.$handler.click(function(){
							self.options.reach.call(self);
						});
					};
					this.bind();
					this.unbind = function(){
						self.$handler
							.unbind("click", self.bind)
					};
					escaped	= true;
				}
			}
		});
	});
</script>

<script>
        $(window).load(function () {

            app.replay_accordion();
            app.replay_prog("#widget-replay-video", "#widget-prog-list", "/replay/emission/", "/ar/replay/last/", true);

            /*
             click on the last modified media */
            var cat_type = "0";
            var latest_tag_slug = "sayidate-shopping";

            if (parseInt(cat_type) <= 0) {
                /*
                 emission */
                $("[data-href='programs']").trigger("click");
                $("#prog-tab-control").trigger("click");
                $("#prog-tab-content .article").first().trigger("click");
            } else {
                /*
                 journal */
                $("[data-href='news']").trigger("click");
                $("#journal-tabs a[data-href='" + latest_tag_slug + "']").trigger("click");
                $("#journal-tabs")
                        .next(".tabs-container")
                        .find(".tab-content.active").first().find(".accordion-item").first()
                        .find(".accordion-handler").click()
                        .next(".accordion-content")
                        .find(".article").first().click();
                
            }
        });
    </script>

<script>
    var app = app || {};

    app.webroot = "http://2m.ma/ar/";
    app.search_url = "/ar/search/?q=";

    
    var can_open_the_popup = false;
    var adjust_user_pop_up = function () {
        $("[id^='popup-user-']").css("visibility", "hidden").each(function () {
            var offset = Math.max(($(window).height() - $(this).innerHeight()) / 2, 70);
            $(this).css({
                "margin-top": offset, "visibility": "visible"
            });
        });
    };

    app.user_pop_up_toggle = function (el) {
        var $el = $(el).parents("[id^='popup-user-']");
        $el.hide().siblings().show();
        return false;
    }

    app.open_auth = function () {
        if(!can_open_the_popup)
            return;
        $("body").addClass("user-auth-overlayed");
        $("#overlay").fadeIn("fast");

        adjust_user_pop_up();

        $("[id^='popup-user-']").hide(); // hide all
        $("#popup-user-auth").show();

        return false;
    };

    app.close_user_box = function () {
        $("body").removeClass("user-auth-overlayed");
        $("#overlay").fadeOut("fast");
        return false;
    }

    $(function(){
        $("#open_user_popup").click(function(e){
            e.preventDefault();
            app.open_auth();
        });
        $(window).load(function () {
            adjust_user_pop_up();
            $("[id^='popup-user-']").hide();
            can_open_the_popup = true;
        }).resize(adjust_user_pop_up);
    });
    

    $(function () {
        $("#alert").click(function () {
            $(this).slideUp("fast");
        });
        $("#alert").delay(7000).slideUp("fast");
    });

    if (app.is_desktop) {
        app.ch_city({
            prayer: '/ar/widget/prayer/0/', weather: '/ar/widget/weather/0/'
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function(){
        var capcthaCallBack = function(){
            for(var _captcha in captchas)
                captchas[_captcha] = captchas[_captcha].call(this);
        };
        capcthaCallBack();
    });
</script>
